1b1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya la li ta bi sta ra nA ma ma hA yA na sU tra/bod skad du/
1b2(mdo sna tshogs): 'phags pa rgya cher rol pa zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo/bam po dang po//sangs rgyas dang byang chub 
1b3(mdo sna tshogs): sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 
1b4(mdo sna tshogs): 'das mnyan yod na/rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal mgon med zas sbyin gyi kun dga' ra ba na/dge slong khri 
1b5(mdo sna tshogs): nyis stong gi dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po la 'di lta ste/tsho dang ldan pa kun shes kau N+Ti n+ya dang /tshe dang 
1b6(mdo sna tshogs): ldan pa rta thul dang /tshe dang ldan pa rlangs pa dang /tshe dang ldan pa ming chen dang /tshe dang ldan pa 
1b7(mdo sna tshogs): bzang po dang /tshe dang ldan pa grags sbyin dang /tshe dang ldan pa dri ma med dang /tshe dang ldan pa lag 
1b8(mdo sna tshogs): bzangs dang /tshe dang ldan pa gang po dang /tshe dang ldan ba ba lang bdag dang /tshe dang ldan pa lteng rgyas 
2a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//
2b1(mdo sna tshogs): thams cad rab tu rdzogs pa sha stag ste/'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po byams pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen po gzungs kyi dbang phyug rgyal po dang /byang chub 
2b2(mdo sna tshogs): sems dpa' sems dpa' che po seng ge'i tog dang /byang chub sems pa'i sems dpa' chen po don grub blo gros dang /byang chub sems de dpa' sems dpa' chen pa spyod pa rab tu zhi ba'i blo gros dang /
2b3(mdo sna tshogs): byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po so so yang dag par rig pa thob pa dang /byang chub sem dpa' sems dpa' chen mo brtson 'grus rtag par sbyor dang /byang chub sems sems dpa' sems dpa'
2b4(mdo sna tshogs): chen po snying rje chen po sems pa dang /de dag la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa' sum khri nyis stong dang yang thabs gcig go/de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das mnyan yod kyi grong khyer chen po na/'khor bzhi dang /
2b5(mdo sna tshogs): rgyal po dang /rgyal po'i sras dang /rgyal po'i blon po chen po dang /chags 'og gi rgyal phran dang /rgyal po'i zhal pas 'bring ba dang /rgyal rigs dang /bram ze dang /tshong dpon dang /khyim bdag dang /blon po dang /
2b6(mdo sna tshogs): 'khor gyi mi dang /grong khyer pa dang /yul gyi mi dang /mu stegs can dang /dge sbyong dang /bram ze dang /spyod pa pa dang /kun tu rgyu sna tshogs kyis rim gro byas/bla mar byas/sti stang du byas/mchod pa 
2b7(mdo sna tshogs): byas so||bcom ldan 'das ni bza' ba dang /bca' ba dang /myang ba bsod cing mang ba dang /na bza' dang /bsod snyoms dang /gzims cha dang /snyun gyi gsos sman dang /yo byad rung ba brnyes shing /bcom
2b8(mdo sna tshogs): ldan 'das ni brnyes pa dam pa dang /grags pa dam pa brnyes kyang pad ma la chu sa mi gos pa ltar thams cad du ma chags ma gos par bzhugs so//bcom ldan 'das kyi grags pa dang /sgra dang /
3a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//snyan pa ni 'di skad du/bcom ldan 'das de ni de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rig pa dang zhabs su ldan pa/bde bar gshegs pa/'jig 
3a2(mdo sna tshogs): rten mkhyen pa/skyes bu 'dul ba'i kha lo sgyur ba bla na med pa/lha dang mi rnams kyi ston pa/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das shes bya bar 'jig rten du mngon par grags par gyur to//de ni spyan lnga dang ldan pa 
3a3(mdo sna tshogs): ste/'jig rten 'di dang 'jig rten pha rol lha dang bcas pa/tshangs pa dang bcas pa/dge sbyong dang bram zer bcas pa'i skye dgu lha dang mir bcas pa dag/rang gis mngon par mkhyen pas mngon du 
3a4(mdo sna tshogs): mdzad nas nye bar bsgrubs te rab tu ston to//de thog mar dge ba/bar du dge ba/tha mar dge ba don bzang po tshig 'bru bzang po/ma 'dres pa/yongs su rdzogs pa/yongs su dag pa/yongs su dkar ba'i chos 
3a5(mdo sna tshogs): ston te/tshangs pa'i spyod pa yang dag par ston to//de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyis nam gyi gud thun la/sangs rgyas kyi rgyan bkod pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs par gyur to//
3a6(mdo sna tshogs): bcom ldan 'das kyis sangs rgyas kyi rgyan bkod pa/zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin/de la snyoms par zhugs ma thag tu/skad cig de nyid la bcom ldan 'das kyi dbu'i gtsug gi gtsug tor gyi gseb nas/
3a7(mdo sna tshogs): sngon gyi sangs rgyas rjes su dran par bya ba la chags pa med pa'i ye shes snang ba zhes bya ba'i 'od zer byung ste/des gnas gtsang ma'i lha'i gnas thams cad snang bar byas nas/lha'i bu dbang phyug 
3a8(mdo sna tshogs): chen po la sogs pa lha'i bu tshad med pa dag yang dag par bskul to//de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'od zer gyi tshogs de las bskul ba'i tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag kyang byung ngo //ye shes 'od ldan 
3b1(mdo sna tshogs): mun bcom 'od mdzad pa//'od bzang gzi brjid dam pa dri med pa//sku lus rab zhi thugs dge zhi ba po//shAkya'i seng ge thub dang phrad par gyis//ye shes rgya mtsho mthu chen dag pa ste//chos kyi mnga' bdag 
3b2(mdo sna tshogs): kun mkhyen thub pa'i dbang //mi dang lhas mchod lha yi lhar gyur pa//chos la rang byung mnga' dbang mdzad la rton//gang zhig gdul dka'i sems la mnga' mdzad de//gang zhig bdud kyi snyi las grol 
3b3(mdo sna tshogs): ba'i thugs//de 'dir mthong thos don med ma yin gyis//rnam grol mthar phyin zhi ba'i drung du deng //mtshungs med chos la snang bar gyur pa ste//mun sel tshul bzang ston par rab mdzad pa//sangs 
3b4(mdo sna tshogs): rgyas spyod zhi mkhyen pa dpag med pa//de yi drung du dang ba mchog gis deng //de ni sman pa'i rgyal po bdud rtsi gtong //de ni smra ba dpa' bo tshogs ngan 'joms//de ni chos kyi rtsa lag don dam mkhas//de ni 
3b5(mdo sna tshogs): 'dren pa bla med lam ston pa'o//gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha'i bu de dag sngon gyi sangs rgyas rjes su dran par bya ba la chags pa med pa'i ye shes snang ba'i 'od zer des phog cing tshigs su bcad pa 
3b6(mdo sna tshogs): rnam pa de lta bu de dag gis kyang bskul ma thag tu rab tu zhi ba'i ting nge 'dzin de las langs nas sangs rgyas kyi mthus bskal pa grangs med tshang med//bgrang ba las shin tu 'das pa dag las 'das pa'i 
3b7(mdo sna tshogs): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams rjes su dran zhing /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/de dag gi sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi yon tan bkod pa gang yin pa dag dang /'khor gyi dkyil 'khor gang yin pa dag 
3b8(mdo sna tshogs): dang /chos bstan pa gang yin pa de dag thams cad kyang rjes su dran par gyur to//de nas de'i nub mo mi nyal tsam na gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha yi bu dbang phyug ces bya ba dang /dbang phyug chen po dang /dga' bo dang /shin tu 
4a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//dga' bo dang /tsan dan dang /mchod byas dang /rab zhi dang /dul ba rab zhi dbang phyug la sogs pa de dag dang /gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha'i bu gzhan shin tu mang po kha dog 'phags shing 
4a2(mdo sna tshogs): shin tu 'phags pa dang ldan pa dag gis/lha'i snang bas rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal thams cad snang bar byas te/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der dong nas phyin pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo 
4a3(mdo sna tshogs): bos phyag 'tshal te phyogs gcig tu 'khod do//phyogs gcig tu 'khod nas kyang gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha yi bu de dag gis bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das rgya cher 
4a4(mdo sna tshogs): rol pa zhes bgyi ba'i chos kyi rnam grangs mdo sde shin tu rgyas pa chen po bsdus pa/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba yang dag par bstan pa/dga' ldan gyi pho brang dam pa nas 'bab pa dang 
4a5(mdo sna tshogs): ched du dgongs te lhu las su 'jug pa'i rnam par rol pa dang /mngal gyi gnas kyi khyad par kun tu bstan pa/rigs bzang por skye ba'i sa'i mthu kun tu bstan pa/byis pa'i spyod pa thams cad las yon tan 
4a6(mdo sna tshogs): gyi khyad par gyis shin tu 'da' ba bstan pa/'jig rten pa'i bzo'i gnas dang /las kyi gnas dang /yi ge dang /grangs dang /lag rtsis dang /rtsis dang /ral gri 'thabs dang /'phong dang /stobs dang /gyad 
4a7(mdo sna tshogs): kyi 'dzin stangs rnams sems can thams cad las khyad par du 'phags pa kun tu bstan pa/btsun mo'i 'khor dang /yul gyi longs spyod dang /yongs su spyod pa kun tu bstan pa/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod 
4a8(mdo sna tshogs): pa thams cad kyi rgyu 'thun pas yongs su grub pa'i 'bras bu brnyed pa yongs su brjod pa/byang chub sems dpa' rnam par rol pa dang /bdud kyi dkyil 'khor thams cad rnam par 'jig pa bstan pa/
4b1(mdo sna tshogs): de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs dang /mi 'jigs pa bsdus pa/sangs rgyas kyi chos chad ma mchis pa nges par bstan pa/sngon gyi de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis sngon chad gsungs pa mchis 
4b2(mdo sna tshogs): te/'di lta ste/bcom ldan 'das pad ma'i bla ma dang /chos kyi tog dang /mar me mdzad dang /yon tan gyi tog dang /yon tan 'byung gnas dang /'od zer chen po dang /drang srong lha dang /dpal 
4b3(mdo sna tshogs): gyi gzi brjid dang /bden pa'i tog dang /rdo rje mkhregs dang /thams cad zil gyis gnon dang /gser mdog dang /shin tu mthor gshegs dang /byi ru'i rgya mtsho dang /me tog gi tog dang /
4b4(mdo sna tshogs): gzugs bzang dang /spyan bzang dang /drang srong sped dang /dmag tshogs las rgyal dang /mtho ba dang /me tog rgyas pa dang /mdzod spu gzi brjid dang /shin tu rgyas dang /'od zer bzang po dang /
4b5(mdo sna tshogs): bkra shis ldan dang /blta na bzang dang /seng ge'i gzi brjid chen po dang /blo gros brtan pas byin dang /sos ka'i spos dri dang /bden pa'i chos grags rgya chen dang /'od ldan dang /skar rgyal dang /
4b6(mdo sna tshogs): 'jig rten mdzes dang /'od rgya chen dang /rin chen grags dang /drag shul gzi brjid dang /rin chen gzi brjid dang /tshangs pa'i gzi brjid dang /sgra snyan dang /me tog bzang po dang /ri rab me tog 
4b7(mdo sna tshogs): dang /rab tu yid 'ong dbyangs dang /dge ba tshul bzangs dang /rab tu 'dzum pa'i spyan dang /yon tan phung po dang /'brug sgra dang /kha dog mdzes dang /tshe'i gzi brjid dang /dom bag glang po'i 
4b8(mdo sna tshogs): 'gros dang /'jig rten mngon par smon dang /dgra las rgyal dang /yang dag mchod dang /rnam par gzigs dang /gtsug tor can dang /thams cad skyob dang /'khor ba 'jig dang /gser thub 
5a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//
5b1(mdo sna tshogs): gdan bshams pa la bzhugs so//bzhugs nas kyang bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong rnams la bka' stsal pa/dge slong dag ma dang 'dir nga'i drung du gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha'i bu dbang phyug ces 
5b2(mdo sna tshogs): bya ba dang /dbang phyug chen po dang /dga' bo dang /shin tu dga' bo dang /tsan dan dang /mchod byas dang /rab zhi dang /dul ba rab zhi dbang phyug la sogs pa de dag dang gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyis lha'i bu gzhan 
5b3(mdo sna tshogs): shin tu mang po zhes bya ba nas/de nyid du mi snang bar gyur ces bya ba'i bar du snga ma bzhin du gsungs so//de nas byang chub sems dpa' de dag dang /nyan thos chen po de dag gis 
5b4(mdo sna tshogs): bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud de/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das rgya cher rol pa'i chos kyi rnam grangs 
5b5(mdo sna tshogs): legs par bstan du gsol/de ni skye bo mang po la sman pa dang /skye bo mang po la bde ba dang /'jig rten la thugs brtse bar mdzad pa dang /skye bo phal po che dang /lha dang 
5b6(mdo sna tshogs): mi rnams dang /da ltar dang ma 'ongs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi don dang /sman pa dang bde bar 'gyur ro//zhes gsol pa dang /bcom 
5b7(mdo sna tshogs): ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' chen po de dag dang /nyan thos pa chen po de dag dang /lha dang mi dang lha ma yin dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten la thugs brtse 
5b8(mdo sna tshogs): phyir cad mi gsung bas gnang ngo //de la 'di skad ces bya ste/dge slong dag kyang ni mngar sum 'dir//gnas pa bzang pos bde bar gnas gyur cing //nyon mongs med 
6a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//par rab tu gnas gyur la//rtse gcig sems su mnyam par bzhag drung du//rdzu 'phrul che zhing kha dog bzad ldan la//dri med dpal 'bar lha yi bu lhags nas//
6a2(mdo sna tshogs): dpal gyis rgyal byed tshal 'di snang byas te//dga' bas nga yi gan du mngon par lhags//dbang phyug dbang phyug chen po tsan dan dang //dga' bo rab tu sems zhi mchod byas dang //
6a3(mdo sna tshogs): shin tu dga' bo zhi ba lha yi bu//bye ba lha mang de dang de dag rnams//de yi zhabs la phyag 'tshal bskor byas te//nga yi mdun du 'dir ni 'khod par gyur//sor mo bcu yis thal 
6a4(mdo sna tshogs): sbyar gus bcas par//de dag 'dir ni nga la gsol ba btab//'jig rten kun la sman pa'i don srid du//rgyal ba kun gyis sngon chad gang gsungs pa//shin tu rgyas mdo 
6a5(mdo sna tshogs): gleng gzhi chen po 'di//thub pa 'dod chags bsal bas de ring yang //byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs kun gzung bzhed pas//phas kyi rgol dang bdud rnams 'dul mdzad la//
6a6(mdo sna tshogs): theg pa chen po mchog 'di rab gsung zhing //thub pa de slad legs par bshad du gsol//lha yi tshogs kyis gsol ba de snang phyir//mi gsung gyur pas gnang ba mdzad pas na//
6a7(mdo sna tshogs): thams cad tshim zhing rab dga' mgu gyur nas//dga' ba thob pas me tog sil ma gtor//de phyir dge slong shin du rgyas pa'i mdo//'jig rten kun la phan pa'i don gyi phyir//
6a8(mdo sna tshogs): de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyis sngon bshad pa//gleng gzhi chen po khyed kun 'dir nyon cig/gleng gzhi'i le'u ste dang po'o////
6b1(mdo sna tshogs): dge slong dag de la chos kyi rnam grangs shin du rgyas pa chen po'i mdo rgya cher rol pa zhes bya ba de gang zhe na//dge slong dag 'di la byang chub sems dpa' dga' ldan gyi gnas mchog na gnas 
6b2(mdo sna tshogs): pa/mchod par bya bas mchod pa/dbang bskur ba thob pa/lha brgya stong gis bstod cing bkur te brjod cing bsngags pa/bsam pa thob pa/smon lam gyis yang dag par 
6b3(mdo sna tshogs): 'phags pa/sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad yang dag par thob pa'i blo dang ldan pa/ye shes kyi mig shin tu rgyas shing yongs su dag pa dang ldan pa/dran pa dang /blo gros dang /
6b4(mdo sna tshogs): rtogs pa dang /khrel yod pa dang /dga' ba 'bar ba'i blo rlabs po che dang ldan pa/sbyin pa dang /tshul khrims dang /bzod pa dang /brtson 'grus dang /bsam gtan 
6b5(mdo sna tshogs): dang /shes rab dang /thabs la mkhas pa chen po'i dam pa'i pha rol du son pa/tshangs pa'i lam byams pa chen po dang /snying rje chen po dang /dga' ba chen po dang /btang 
6b6(mdo sna tshogs): snyoms chen po la mkhas pa/mngon par shes pa dang /rig pa chen pos sgrib pa med cing chags pa med pa'i ye shes mthong ba mngon du gyur pa/dran pa nye bar gzhag pa dang /
6b7(mdo sna tshogs): yang dag par spong ba dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa dang /dbang po dang /stobs dang /byang chub kyi yan lag dang /lam dang /byang chub kyi phyogs kyi chos thams cad shin 
7a1(mdo sna tshogs): tu yongs su rdzogs pa'i mthar phyin pa/bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs dpag tu med pa'i mtshan dang dpe byad bzang pos lus legs par brgyan pa/yun ring 
7a2(mdo sna tshogs): por rjes su 'brang ba dang ldan pa/ji skad du smras pa de ltar byed pas logs pa med pa'i tshig gi las yang dag par smra ba/drang zhing ma yol/gya gyu med pa'i yid thogs pa med pa dang ldan 
7a3(mdo sna tshogs): pa/nga rgyal dang /rgyags pa dang /dregs pa dang /'jigs pa dang /zhum pa thams cad med pa/sems can thams cad la sems mnyam pa/sangs rgyas bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong dpag tu med 
7a4(mdo sna tshogs): pa la bsnyen bkur byas pa/byang chub sems dpa' bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong mang pos ngor blta zhing blta ba'i bzhin dang ldan pa/brgya byin dang tshangs pa dang /dbang phyug chen po dang /'jig rten 
7a5(mdo sna tshogs): skyong ba dang /lha dang /klu dang /gnod sbyin dang /dri za dang /lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' lding dang /srin po rnams kyis mngon par dga' bar bya ba'i grags pa dang ldan pa/tshig thams cad rab 
7a6(mdo sna tshogs): tu phye ste bstan pa la thogs pa med pa'i so so yang dag par rig pa'i 'jug pa'i shes pa la mkhas pa/sangs rgyas thams cad kyis gsungs pa gzung ba la dran pa'i snod 'khrul pa med pa 
7a7(mdo sna tshogs): dang /gzungs mtha' yas mu med pa rab tu thob pa/dran pa nye bar gzhag pa dang /yang dag par spong ba dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa dang /dbang po dang /stobs dang /byang chub kyi yan 
7a8(mdo sna tshogs): lag dang /lam dang /shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang /thabs mkhas pa'i chos rin po che dang /bsod nams kyis yang dag par bsgrubs pa'i chos kyi grub chen po'i ded dpon chen po 
7b1(mdo sna tshogs): chu bo bzhi'i pha rol tu 'gro ba'i bsam pa can/bdud dang phyer rgol ba bcom pa phas kyis rgol ba thams cad shin tu tshar bcad pa/g.yul ngo'i thog ma la rab tu 'dug pa/nyon mongs pa'i dgra'i 
7b2(mdo sna tshogs): tshogs 'jom pa/ye shes mchog gi rdo rje'i mtshon sra ba dang ldan pa/byang chub kyi sems kyi rtsa ba las snying rje chen po'i chu bo lhag pa'i bsam pas skyes pa zab cing brtan pa dang /brtson 
7b3(mdo sna tshogs): 'grus kyi chos kyi chus mngon par brlan pa/thabs mkhas pa'i snying po dang ldan pa/byang chub gyi yan lag dang /bsam gtan gyi ze ba can/ting nge 'dzin gyi ze 'bru dang ldan pa/yon tan 
7b4(mdo sna tshogs): gyi tshogs rgya chen po'i mtsho las skyes pa/nga rgyal dang dregs pa'i gdungs pa med pa'i zla bas dri ma med pa'i 'dab ma rgyas pa/tshul khrims dang /thos pa dang /bag yod pa'i gsung 
7b5(mdo sna tshogs): gi dang phyogs bcur thogs pa med par ldang ba/'jig rten na shes pa'i thu bor gyur pa/'jig rten gyi chos brgyad kyis ma gos pa/skyes bu chen po'i pad ma/bsod 
7b6(mdo sna tshogs): nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs kyi dri zhim po 'byung ba/shes rab dang ye shes kyi nyi ma'i 'od zer gyis mig shin tu rnam par dag pa'i pad ma 'dab ma brgya pa rgyas pa/rdzu 'phrul 
7b7(mdo sna tshogs): gyi rkang pa bzhi mchog tu mgyogs pa'i shugs dang ldan pa/'phags pa'i bden pa bzhi'i sder mo dang mche ba shin tu rno ba/tshangs pa'i gnas pa bzhi'i 
7b8(mdo sna tshogs): so drang bsnyer ba/bsdu ba'i dngos po bzhis shin tu bsdus pa'i mgo dang ldan pa/rten cing 'brel bar 'byung ba yan lag bcu gnyis pa khong du chud pas rim gyis 
8a1(mdo sna tshogs): 'phags pa'i lus dang ldan pa/byang chub kyi phyogs kyi chos sum bcu rtsa bdun yang dag par yongs su rdzogs pas ral pa can du byas shing rig pa dang /ye shes kyi ze ba can/
8a2(mdo sna tshogs): rnam par thar pa'i sgo gsum gyi kha gdangs pa/zhi gnas dang lhag mthong shin du rnam par dag pa'i mig dang ldan pa/bsam gtan dang /rnam par thar pa dang /ting nge 'dzin dang /snyoms 
8a3(mdo sna tshogs): par 'jug pa'i ri sul gyi phug na gnas pa/spyod lam bzhi dang /'dul ba'i nags tshal dang /nags 'dab nas skyes pa'i lus dang ldan pa/stobs bcu dang /mi 'jigs pa bzhi dang /nye ba'i 
8a4(mdo sna tshogs): stobs dang ldan pa/skye ba dang 'jig pa'i 'jigs pa dang /spu g.yo ba med pa/rtsal la zhum pa med pa/mu stegs can gyi bong bu dang ri dgas mang po'i tshogs rab tu 'joms pa/bdag med par 
8a5(mdo sna tshogs): sgra brjod pa'i seng ge'i nga ro chen po sgrog pa/skyes bu seng ge dang /rnam par grol ba dang /bsam gtan gyi dkyil 'khor las shes rab kyi 'od dang /'od zer gyis mu stegs byed srin bu me khyer

232b6(mdo sna tshogs): tog chen po dang ldan pa'i rgyal po zhes bya'o//'od zer chad med cing mtha' yas pa zhes bya'o//snang ba chen po kun tu ston pa zhes bya'o//dris pa thams cad lung bstan pa ston pa la rmongs pa mi mnga' ba zhes
